- **loki**: Loki is a horizontally scalable log aggregation system built by Grafana Labs. Unlike traditional log platforms that index the full text of every log line, Loki indexes only metadata labels, making it significantly cheaper to operate. It integrates natively with Grafana for querying and visualization, and uses LogQL — a query language inspired by PromQL — to filter, parse, and aggregate log streams. ([TerminalSkills/skills](https://github.com/TerminalSkills/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/loki))
